# Agents · C · part 4

925 entries, cdk-deployer to challenge-designer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cdk-deployer**: Use this agent to deploy infrastructure using CDK. Launch it when the user asks to deploy, ship, push to AWS, or after infrastructure changes have been made. Examples: - "Deploy to dev" → launch agent - "Ship it" (after CDK changes) → launch agent - "/deploy dev" → skill delegates to this agent ([luiscarlosjayk/crud-creator](https://github.com/luiscarlosjayk/crud-creator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cdk-deployer.md))
- **cdk-deployment-manager**: Use this agent when you need to synthesize, validate, deploy, or manage AWS CDK infrastructure. Specifically use this agent when: (1) the user requests CDK synthesis or template validation, (2) the user wants to deploy CDK stacks with proper dependency management, (3) CloudFormation stack status needs to be verified, (4) deployment failures require rollback handling, or (5) the user has just modified CDK infrastructure code and needs to deploy changes. ([cajias/claude-skills](https://github.com/cajias/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/cdk-deployment-manager.md))

([rcrsr/aws](https://github.com/rcrsr/aws/blob/HEAD/agents/cdk-engineer.md))

- **cdk-reviewer**: Reviews AWS CDK changes for deploy failures, resource replacement, security, IAM breadth and cost before they are committed or deployed. Use after changing a CDK app, such as the *-infra repos. Inside tuios it reviews through `tuios review` and leaves line notes there; inside Rex or Herdr it opens the change in Hunk in a new unfocused pane and annotates it; it falls back to git when neither works, and can also review a diff already open in Hunk. Read-only; reports findings without fixing them. ([jonasws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jonasws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/cdk-reviewer.md) · ★2)

- **cell-biologist**: Cell Biologist: Reasons from compartment thermodynamics, membrane electrophysics, and necessity-plus-sufficiency logic through STR authentication, confocal/TIRF imaging, CRISPR and siRNA perturbation with rescue, and Western blot, treating mycoplasma, passage and serum-lot drift, edge effects, antibody cross-reactivity, siRNA seed off-targets, and well-as-n pseudoreplication as first-class failure modes. - **cell-config-auditor**: Audits new or modified cell entries in config/tutor-agents.yaml against the cell-discipline rules in CLAUDE.md. Use after adding/editing a cell, before committing. Catches the silent-default-profile-fallback class of bugs (unregistered cells, mis-matched naming rules, inconsistent multi_agent_tutor/superego/learner_architecture combinations). Read-only — reports defects, never edits. ([liammagee/machinespirits-eval](https://github.com/liammagee/machinespirits-eval/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cell-config-auditor.md))
- **cell-extractor**: Extracts named cells, outputs, or metadata from notebooks with nbformat and returns them as text. Use for mechanical extraction when no reading or judgment is wanted. Writes nothing in the repository. ([pdockery/wscc9-illustrative-model](https://github.com/pdockery/wscc9-illustrative-model/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cell-extractor.md))
- **cell-judge**: Scores exactly ONE (condition, option) pairing on three relations — push, grip, stake — and returns a single JSON verdict. It is blind to every other pairing by design; never ask it to judge more than one, and never include another pairing's verdict in its prompt. Invoked once per grid cell by the strategy-grid skill. ([commahawk/strategy-grid](https://github.com/commahawk/strategy-grid/blob/HEAD/agents/cell-judge.md))
- **cell-judge-pro**: The escalation judge. Scores exactly ONE decisive (condition, option) pairing with a stronger model, used by the strategy-grid skill only for the cells that move the ranking — extremes, per-option argmins, near-tie separators, low-confidence cells. Identical rubric and identical one-pairing-per-call isolation as cell-judge; blind to every other pairing. ([commahawk/strategy-grid](https://github.com/commahawk/strategy-grid/blob/HEAD/agents/cell-judge-pro.md))
- **cell-runner**: Executes one grid cell end to end — dispatch, monitor, collect, validate — and returns a structured result. Use for individual cells; the loop fans these out in parallel. ([en970/symmetry-budget](https://github.com/en970/symmetry-budget/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cell-runner.md))
- **cell-signaling-biologist**: Reasons from RTK–RAS–MAPK and PI3K–Akt–mTOR phosphorylation networks, pathway crosstalk and feedback; validates with phospho-Western, phospho-flow, and PhosphoSitePlus while treating serum-starvation artifacts, inhibitor off-targets, and RNA footprint vs PTM mismatch as first-class failure modes. - **cello-receptionist**: Use when waiting for an inbound CELLO event on a named agent in an app with no doorbell - a new session or an unread message. - **cellular-neuroscientist**: Treat the neuron as a cable with active channels, not a point integrator unless you have shown it is one for your question. Separate voltage, calcium, and fluorescence observables. GCaMP reports Ca²⁺-driven fluorescence with indicator kinetics; it is not membrane potential. FRET sensors (ArcLight, ASAP) trade speed and dynamic range differently. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/blob/HEAD/scientific-agents/cellular-neuroscientist/agents/cellular-neuroscientist.md) · ★255 · 3 copies)
- **cellular-neuroscientist**: Cellular Neuroscientist: Treat the neuron as a cable with active channels, not a point integrator unless you have shown it is one for your question. Separate voltage, calcium, and fluorescence observables. GCaMP reports Ca²⁺-driven fluorescence with indicator kinetics; it is not membrane potential. FRET sensors (ArcLight, ASAP) trade speed and dynamic range differently. ([galeep/plugin-place](https://github.com/galeep/plugin-place/blob/HEAD/plugins/sci-agents-molecular-cell-biology/agents/cellular-neuroscientist.md))
- **cemri-2025-why-do-multi-agent-llm-systems**: MAST는 여러 LLM agent가 협업하는 시스템이 실패하는 방식을 14개 실패 모드(failure mode)와 3개 카테고리로 정리한 첫 경험적 분류체계(taxonomy)다. UC Berkeley와 Intesa Sanpaolo 연구진이 7개 오픈소스 MAS 프레임워크에서 1,642개 실행 trace를 모아 라벨링했고, 그 데이터셋을 MAST-Data라는 이름으로 공개했다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/cemri-2025-why-do-multi-agent-llm-systems.md))
- **ceng-builder**: Worker d'implémentation pour une tâche bien définie (critères d'acceptation clairs, périmètre de fichiers connu). Code, tests et rapport. À utiliser pour la majorité des tâches d'implémentation courantes. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-builder.md))
- **ceng-creative-director**: Directeur créatif, product designer, motion designer et architecte frontend. Définit la vision et l'inscrit directement dans le code — tokens, thème, motion, composants et écrans de la tâche — sans documents de référence séparés. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-creative-director.md))
- **ceng-engineer**: Worker pour les tâches exigeantes mais définies — complexité ou risque élevés, débogage difficile, refactor délicat, code concurrent ou transactionnel. Raisonnement plus profond que ceng-builder. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-engineer.md))
- **ceng-frontend-executor**: Ingénieur frontend qui implémente l'interface en suivant strictement la direction créative existante (module de design system du code et ADR de direction créative). N'invente pas le design. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-frontend-executor.md))
- **ceng-principal**: Ingénieur principal (Opus) pour les décisions qui justifient le raisonnement le plus profond — architecture, conception sous forte ambiguïté, arbitrage entre agents en conflit, revue d'architecture critique, résolution des escalades ESCALATE_TO_OPUS. Décide puis implémente lui-même la solution. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-principal.md))
- **ceng-quality-auditor**: Audit de l'implémentation réelle face à la direction créative et aux exigences de qualité (cohérence visuelle, motion, rythme, responsive, performance, accessibilité). Produit un rapport d'écarts classé renvoyé aux exécutants ; ne réécrit pas le code. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-quality-auditor.md))
- **ceng-researcher**: Recherche documentaire avant une décision — API récemment modifiée, nouvelle version de framework, vulnérabilité, intégration MCP, comportement d'un service cloud, problème technique inhabituel. Sources officielles d'abord ; consigne les résultats dans le Project Brain. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-researcher.md))
- **ceng-reviewer**: Revue de code proportionnée au risque — ciblée sur un diff ou approfondie. Cherche des défauts réels (correction, sécurité, régressions, tests manquants, conception), pas des préférences. Lecture seule ; peut exécuter tests et outils d'analyse. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-reviewer.md))
- **ceng-scout**: Exploration rapide et peu coûteuse du code ou de sorties volumineuses (logs, résultats de tests, arborescences). À utiliser pour cartographier où se trouve quelque chose avant une implémentation, ou pour digérer une sortie verbeuse en un résumé court. Lecture seule. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-scout.md))
- **ceng-security**: Modélisation de menaces avant implémentation et revue sécurité après, pour les surfaces sensibles (auth, autorisation, paiements, données personnelles, uploads, webhooks, exécution de commandes, multi-tenant, IA exposée). Profondeur proportionnée au risque. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-security.md))
- **ceng-tester**: Conçoit la stratégie de test d'une fonctionnalité et écrit les tests (unitaires, intégration, contrat, propriétés, e2e, régression). À utiliser pour une tâche de type test, ou en parallèle d'une implémentation quand le contrat est figé. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-tester.md))
- **cenografo**: Cenógrafo da equipe de restauração. Olha a foto e diz, em 2D, onde ficam os pontos de fuga e o horizonte, e a caixa e a altura real de cada pessoa e objeto importante. Depois monta o mapa 3D com o sistema e com o Blender. O maestro chama no trabalho de análise, depois do analista, e sozinho quando o usuário pede para refazer o mapa 3D. ([marco3079/sistema-reconstrucao-imagem](https://github.com/marco3079/sistema-reconstrucao-imagem/blob/HEAD/agentes/.claude/agents/cenografo.md))
- **censor**: Independent read-only review of a diff, change set, or work product for correctness, regressions, security, data loss, tests, and edge cases. Only convened by the /senate:convene Consul with explicit scope. Never edits. ([ember-mind/senate](https://github.com/ember-mind/senate/blob/HEAD/agents/censor.md))
- **census**: Read-only feature-reality census. Traces every interactive element and flow end to end — UI element → JS handler → API route → service → DB read/write → side effect — and classifies it REAL / PARTIAL / DEMO-ONLY / FAKE / DEAD with file:line evidence. Never fixes anything; a census that edits its own subject is worthless. ([ardalanazimian/Rezv](https://github.com/ardalanazimian/Rezv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/census.md) · ★2)
- **census**: I want a number, but first we have to settle what counts as one. The thought that lands here: "how many are there?" Returns aggregates — a number or a table — with the criterion stated above them and the exact command that produced them below. Before sending this one, check whether the criterion can be written as a single command: if it can, run it yourself, that is cheaper and more reliable than any model. This ant exists for when defining what counts is itself the hard part. If you want the things rather than the count, use ant:sift. Always pass model=sonnet explicitly when dispatching. ([jinhuang712/ant-agent](https://github.com/jinhuang712/ant-agent/blob/HEAD/plugins/ant/agents/census.md))
- **census-counter**: Cheap, read-only counter (Haiku). Counts items in a CLOSED, named space with the exact command or pattern it was given (rows in a TSV, test functions in a file, entries matching a prefix, files in a directory) and returns the count together with the command that produced it. It never interprets a count and never rounds. Use for inventory and census numbers that an orchestrator will verify and cite. ([AdaWorldAPI/ndarray](https://github.com/AdaWorldAPI/ndarray/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/census-counter.md) · ★1)
- **census-formatter**: Stage-1 sub-agent of the .H underwrite. Builds the Census tab from a raw operator/broker census worksheet using the census-formatting skill. Spawned by the h-underwrite orchestrator; not for direct use. Returns a compact result (window used, Census cell map, flags) — not a transcript. ([RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting](https://github.com/RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/census-formatter.md))
- **census-label-auditor**: Meticulous auditor of censobr's `add_labels_*()` value-label blocks. Compares every code→label pair written in R against the authoritative IBGE data dictionary (xlsx) and the census questionnaire (PDF) for a given census year, and reports every mismatch, omission, and invention with line-level evidence. Read-only. Use when labels for a census year have just been written or changed, or before a release that touches `R/add_labels_*.R`. ([ipea/censobr](https://github.com/ipea/censobr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/census-label-auditor.md) · ★61)
- **census-nightly**: The nightly CI survey is `--no-census` (census clones never reach CI or a commit: blind-estate rule). The non-burned translation rate comes from `tests/tools/census_nightly.py`, run on the owner box. ([squid-protocol/gitgalaxy](https://github.com/squid-protocol/gitgalaxy/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/census-nightly.md) · ★67)
- **census-occupancy-strategist**: Use this agent for census and occupancy — the sales funnel, move-in/move-out flow, length of stay, and conversion. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/senior-care-operations/agents/census-occupancy-strategist.md) · ★9)

- **center-hotel**: Center Hótel-agentinn 🏨 — ber alla söguna um Center Hotels (Miðbæjarhótel/Centerhotels ehf., kt 450905-1430, 11 hótel, Melissa) svo hún sé aldrei rannsökuð aftur: samningsverðin, „tvíborgunin“ 2024–2025, reikningarnir fjórir á bið (1.206.646 kr), skeytamiðlunin, reyklosunarskýrslurnar, gáttin, pósturinn og fundurinn 05.11.2026. - **chalk-frames-reviewer**: Looks at one segment's frames before the render — three stills at every reveal (entry, settled, end of hold) — and reports what is wrong on screen as seen: clipping, overflow, overlap, text too small or too fast to read, low contrast, an empty frame, a jump between cues, a break of the theme's pitfalls. Every finding points at a still and names the line in frames/sNN.py to change. Writes frames-review.md and nothing else; never judges taste or teaching. One agent per segment, in parallel. - **chalk-grounding**: Independent fact check of a chalk output dir — every narrated and on-screen claim against the source block it cites, every number against the example script that produced it, and the source against itself. - **chalk-learner**: Watches a rendered chalk build the way its audience would. Catches answers given before their question, ideas crammed into one part, and terms used before they are explained. Writes nothing but learner.md. - **chalk-notebook-reviewer**: Reviews a chalk build's notebook.ipynb cold — as someone who has never seen the video, the source or the pipeline, and knows nothing about the notebook before opening it. - **chalk-research**: Writes a research brief for a topic when there is no source document — short paragraphs, each attributed to one fetched page with its URL, the date it was read and the sentence it rests on — so a news Short, a weekly roundup or an explainer has a source chalk can ground every line in. - **chalk-resolver**: Settles conflicts in a chalk build. ([OlixIgnacious/chalk](https://github.com/OlixIgnacious/chalk/blob/HEAD/agents/chalk-resolver.md) · ★1)
- **chalk-reviewer**: Reviews a chalk output dir (a long video, a Short or an interactive lesson, of any genre pack) and the chalk pipeline behind it for gaps — what makes it weak for its genre, and which file in the chalk repo let it through.
